SELECT
  row_identity_hash,
  event_date,
  event_timestamp_utc,
  event_timestamp_pst,
  page_app_type,
  site_name,
  full_page_name,
  customer_id,
  first_party_id,
  app_instance_id,
  channel_name,
  COALESCE(event_page_view, 0) AS page_view,
  COALESCE(event_link_click, 0) AS link_click
FROM prd_dbi_analytics.silver_digital_interactions.unified_digital_interactions
WHERE event_date = DATE '2026-09-23'                                         -- partition filter
  AND COALESCE(attribute_event_type, '') <> 'Debug Attribute'                -- drop app telemetry
  AND COALESCE(site_name, '') <> 'Expert Assist'                             -- drop care-agent tool
  AND NOT lower(COALESCE(user_agent, '')) RLIKE
      'bot|crawl|spider|slurp|adsbot|storebot|vertexbot|mediapartners'       -- drop known crawlers
LIMIT 1000;
OUTPUT:
row_identity_hash	event_date	event_timestamp_utc	event_timestamp_pst	page_app_type	site_name	full_page_name	customer_id	first_party_id	app_instance_id	channel_name	page_view	link_click

SELECT
  COUNT(participant_key)                                              AS pk_populated,
  SUM(CASE WHEN length(participant_key) = 32 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)      AS pk_32,
  SUM(CASE WHEN length(participant_key) = 31 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)      AS pk_31,
  SUM(CASE WHEN participant_key LIKE 'device_id_%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS pk_device_id,
  SUM(CASE WHEN length(participant_key) NOT IN (31, 32)
            AND participant_key NOT LIKE 'device_id_%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS pk_other,
  COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN length(participant_key) = 31
                      THEN lpad(participant_key, 32, '0') END)        AS pk31_padded_distinct
FROM prd_dbi_analytics.silver_digital_interactions.unified_digital_interactions
WHERE event_date = DATE '2026-09-23';
OUTPUT:
pk_populated	pk_32	pk_31	pk_device_id	pk_other	pk31_padded_distinct
234626667	223579057	10001391	511551	534668	158148

SELECT
  COUNT(*)                          AS rows,
  COUNT(DISTINCT lower(hit_id))     AS distinct_hit_id,
  COUNT(DISTINCT row_identity_hash) AS distinct_row_hash
FROM prd_dbi_analytics.silver_digital_interactions.unified_digital_interactions
WHERE event_date = DATE '2026-09-23';
OUTPUT:
rows	distinct_hit_id	distinct_row_hash
348802553	347466427	348802553

SELECT
  SUM(CASE WHEN try_cast(event_collection_timestamp_utc AS TIMESTAMP) IS NULL
            AND event_collection_timestamp_utc IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS bad_collection_ts,
  SUM(CASE WHEN length(participant_key) = 31 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS pk_31_chars,
  SUM(CASE WHEN participant_key LIKE 'device_id_%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS pk_device_id,
  SUM(CASE WHEN hit_id <> lower(hit_id) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS upper_hit_ids
FROM prd_dbi_analytics.silver_digital_interactions.unified_digital_interactions
WHERE event_date = DATE '2026-09-23';
OUTPUT:
bad_collection_ts	pk_31_chars	pk_device_id	upper_hit_ids
0	10001391	511551	172234011
